HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_001195/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_21d175ccaf1fb04253d6","text":"4. **The visible human conceals an inward account.** `ء ن س B001` presents human presence as visible over against what is hidden or wild. Later, `ق ب ر B002` supplies inward hiddenness and 100:10 places material inside `ص د ر B001`, the bodily chest. Visibility of the named human contrasts with concealment of the human's contents. The packet supplies outer presence and inner enclosures; I infer that the outward subject carries an undisclosed causal interior. `ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ` is therefore read as the visible bearer of a condition that will require inward exposure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001321/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_31b67fb5e9ebbe2b7f91","text":"3. **The problem is selective attachment, not inability to attach.** `ك ن د B001` severs one bond, while 100:8 activates `ح ب ب B002` as clinging love and `ش د د B001` as fastening a bond. The two ayat reverse one another: the relation to `رَبِّهِ` is cut, but the relation to `ٱلْخَيْرِ` is tightly bound. The packet gives severing and fastening; I infer that attachment has been redirected from giver to good. The fixed ayah consequently reads as a reallocation of loyalty rather than emotional emptiness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B006","root_000259/B009","root_000532/B001","root_001321/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3cf29e28a4173ab02f23","text":"3. **False autonomy of the gathered self.** Evidence: ء ن س B006 self/intimate companion; ج م ع B009 wholeness/allness; ر ب ب B001 ownership; ك ن د B002 self-serving withholding. Structural cue: 100:5's gathered center is immediately followed by a possessive relation to Rabb. Role: the human self is an internally gathered companion to itself; rabb asserts external ownership. Abductive move: self-gathering becomes morally corrupt when it denies the owner/nurturer. Reading change: the ayah reads as a critique of self-contained subjectivity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002","root_001321/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_49d5b97eed6564e8dd2b","text":"1. **Human as visible domestic being who severs nurture.** Evidence: ء ن س B001 visible human presence, B003 familiarity/comfort; ر ب ب B002 nurture/completion and B001 lordship/authority; ك ن د B001 severing, B002 ingratitude. Structural cue: the oaths about animals/forces give way to \"the human\" in relation to \"his Lord.\" Role: insān supplies the tamed/familiar creature; rabb supplies the nurturing owner; kanud names the cut. Abductive move: ingratitude is intelligible as a domesticated dependent refusing the relation that made it stable. Reading change: the ayah reads as a relational rupture, not merely a trait-label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6fe6da9f3933d509c16b","text":"The return of رب in 100:11 retrospectively tightened 100:6: the Lord is not just the offended authority but the expert nurturer who knows the dependent from within the relation. 100:10 also made كَنود read as a chest-condition that will be extracted, not only a behavior.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001321/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_796be07729d2781a329f","text":"2. **A barren-soil mechanism for failed reciprocity.** `ك ن د B003` is land that produces nothing. Within this ayah's window, `ر ب ب B002` supplies cultivation-like nurture, `ح ب ب B001` in 100:8 supplies seed-grain, and `خ ي ر B005` supplies gift or beneficence. These branches form giver or cultivator, seed-like favor, receiving ground, and absent yield. The packet supplies the ecological roles but not their direction; I infer that favor is planted and gratitude would be its return. `لَكَنُودٌ` can thus read as nonproductive reception: the human takes sustaining good yet yields no corresponding fruit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8dae70cdf90846f4e4a9","text":"100:8 provides the sharpest correction: `ح ب ب B002` and `ش د د B001` show that the kanud human can cling and bind intensely, so the defect is selective severance rather than incapacity for relation. Then 100:10 exposes the hidden source and 100:11 repeats `ر ب ب`, closing the sequence back on the nurturer whose bond was cut. Together they make both the relational and barren-soil readings more precise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a16a0ece06b957214ba5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:6:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Görünür insan, nankörlüğün kaynağını göğsünde saklayan ve iç hesabı sonradan açılacak bir taşıyıcı olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_001321/B001","root_001321/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_aeb8edc74432d079a3e8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:6:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]],[\"100:6:4\",\"root_001321\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sorun insanı aşama aşama besleyip olgunlaştıran Rabbine karşı sürdürülen ilişkinin kesilmesidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001321/B001","root_001321/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f259d06d6c4f83fb781a","text":"1. **Ingratitude as a severed relation to sustained care.** `ر ب ب B002` supplies repair, nurture, and completion stage by stage; `ك ن د B001` supplies severing, including cutting off gratitude; and `ك ن د B002` supplies forgetting favors and withholding support. The possessive relation in `لِرَبِّهِۦ` places `لَكَنُودٌ` against the one who tends and completes. Nurture is the continuing input, while kanud is the cut in return. The packet supplies those two roles; I infer that sustained favor calls for responsive gratitude. The diagnosis therefore reads as relational rupture with a benefactor, not an isolated negative trait.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B008","root_000532/B013","root_001119/B001","root_001321/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f4674b13bf3c49cec74c","text":"2. **Barren land after withheld rain.** Evidence: ك ن د B003 barren nonproductive land; ر ب ب B008 low/layered cloud and B013 abundant collected water; غ ي ر B001 watering/repair; ن ق ع B002 quenching. Structural cue: the first half is arid dust and heat; the divine side has cloud/water/nurture branches. Role: kanud turns the human into land that yields nothing under care. Abductive move: gratitude should be the \"produce\" of nurture; its absence is barrenness. Reading change: the accusation becomes ecological: the human is nonproductive ground under a sustaining Lord.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000059/B003","root_000532/B002","root_001321/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f9aecf3757ab89bc7362","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:6:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"100:6:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"100:6:4\",\"root_001321\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsanın yakınlık kuran ve bakım içinde olgunlaşan bir varlık oluşu, kendisini istikrara kavuşturan bakıma sırt çevirmesini daha keskin bir bağımlılık ihlali yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
